<a id="words-3001-3100"></a>
## Words 3,001 – 3,100

<a id="w-3001"></a>
### 3001. [abolish](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/abolish) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈbɒlɪʃ/` · **US:** `/əˈbɑːlɪʃ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/abo/aboli/abolish__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/abo/aboli/abolish__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** हटाना, मिटा देना, उन्मूलन करना
- **Examples:**
  - This tax should be abolished.

<a id="w-3002"></a>
### 3002. [abrupt](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/abrupt)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/abrupt)
- **Meaning:** उखड़ा उखड़ा, आकस्मिक, असंगत

<a id="w-3003"></a>
### 3003. [absurd](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/absurd) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əbˈsɜːd/` · **US:** `/əbˈsɜːrd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/abs/absur/absurd__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/abs/absur/absurd__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Meaning:** निरर्थक, बेतुका, असंगत
- **Examples:**
  - That uniform makes the guards look absurd.
  - Of course it's not true, what an absurd idea.

<a id="w-3004"></a>
### 3004. [accelerate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accelerate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əkˈseləreɪt/` · **US:** `/əkˈseləreɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accel/accelerate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accel/accelerate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** चाल बढ़ाना, तेज़ी से बढ़ाना, तेज़ करना
- **Examples:**
  - Inflation continues to accelerate.
  - accelerate something Exposure to the sun can accelerate the ageing process.

<a id="w-3005"></a>
### 3005. [accentuate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accentuate)
- [Oxford Learner's Dictionary Search](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accentuate)
- **Meaning:** जोर दना, जोर देना, अधिक सुस्पष्ट करना

<a id="w-3006"></a>
### 3006. [accessible](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accessible) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əkˈsesəbl/` · **US:** `/əkˈsesəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/acces/accessible__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/acces/accessible__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** उपलब्ध, सुगम्य, गमनीय
- **Examples:**
  - The remote desert area is accessible only by helicopter.
  - accessible to somebody These documents are not accessible to the public.

<a id="w-3007"></a>
### 3007. [accommodate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accommodate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈkɒmədeɪt/` · **US:** `/əˈkɑːmədeɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accom/accommodate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accom/accommodate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** देना, सहायता करना, मिलाना
- **Examples:**
  - The hotel can accommodate up to 500 guests.
  - The aircraft is capable of accommodating 28 passengers.

<a id="w-3008"></a>
### 3008. [accumulate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accumulate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/` · **US:** `/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accum/accumulate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accum/accumulate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** ढेर लगना, जमा करना, जमा होना
- **Examples:**
  - I seem to have accumulated a lot of books.
  - By investing wisely she accumulated a fortune.
  - We have accumulated a great amount of evidence.

### 3009. [accurate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/accurate) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈækjərət/` · **US:** `/ˈækjərət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acc/accur/accurate__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acc/accur/accurate__us_1.mp3)

  - I'm not convinced the reports are accurate.
  - The film is not historically accurate.

### 3010. [acute](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/acute) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/əˈkjuːt/` · **US:** `/əˈkjuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/acu/acute/acute__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/acu/acute/acute__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अत्यधिक, नुकीला, कुशाग्र

<a id="w-3011"></a>
### 3011. [adhere](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/adhere) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ədˈhɪə(r)/` · **US:** `/ədˈhɪr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/adh/adher/adhere__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/adh/adher/adhere__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** पालन करना, समर्थन करना, पालन
- **Examples:**
  - Once in the bloodstream, the bacteria adhere to the surface of the red cells.
  - Clean the surface first, or the paint will not adhere.
  - There was oil adhering to the bird’s feathers.
  - The tiles may not adhere properly if you do not use the correct glue.

- **Examples:**

<a id="w-3023"></a>
### 3023. [allege](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/allege) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/əˈledʒ/` · **US:** `/əˈledʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/all/alleg/allege__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/all/alleg/allege__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बिना सबूत के आरोप लगाना, आरोप लगाना, आरोप  लगाना
- **Examples:**
  - allege (that)… The prosecution alleges (that) she was driving carelessly.
  - it is alleged (that)… It is alleged that he mistreated the prisoners.
  - be alleged to be, have, etc. something He is alleged to have mistreated the prisoners.
  - allege something This procedure should be followed in cases where dishonesty has been alleged.

- **Meaning:** निर्धारित करना, नियत  करना, नियत करना
- **Examples:**
  - allocate something (for something) A large sum has been allocated for buying new books for the library.
  - allocate something (to somebody/something) They intend to allocate more places to mature students this year.
  - More resources are being allocated to the project.
  - allocate somebody/something sth The project is being allocated more resources.

- **Meaning:** शुद्ध करना, स्पष्टीकरण देना, स्पष्ट करना
- **Examples:**
  - clarify something to clarify a situation/problem/issue
  - I hope this clarifies my position.
  - The law on drugs needs to be clarified.
  - Talking to someone has helped clarify my feelings.

### 3142. [consecutive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consecutive) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsekjətɪv/` · **US:** `/kənˈsekjətɪv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsecutive__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsecutive__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** लगातार
- **Examples:**
  - She was absent for nine consecutive days.
  - He is beginning his fourth consecutive term of office.
  - After six consecutive defeats, the team was almost ready to give up.
  - Interest rates rose again for the fifth consecutive month.

<a id="w-3143"></a>
### 3143. [consensus](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consensus) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsensəs/` · **US:** `/kənˈsensəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsensus__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsensus__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सहमति, तालमेल, अनुकूलता
- **Examples:**
  - consensus (about/on something) She is skilled at achieving consensus on sensitive issues.
  - There is a growing consensus of opinion on this issue.
  - an attempt to reach a consensus
  - There now exists a broad political consensus in favour of economic reform.

<a id="w-3144"></a>
### 3144. [consolidate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/consolidate) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsɒlɪdeɪt/` · **US:** `/kənˈsɑːlɪdeɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsolidate__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xcons/xconsolidate__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** मजबूत  बनाना, मजबूत बनाना, मिल जाना
- **Examples:**
  - With this new movie he has consolidated his position as the country's leading director.
  - Italy consolidated their lead with a second goal.

### 3164. [credibility](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/credibility) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˌkredəˈbɪləti/` · **US:** `/ˌkredəˈbɪləti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cre/credi/credibility__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cre/credi/credibility__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** विश्वसनीयता, विश्वस्नीयता
- **Examples:**

  - After the recent scandal, the government has lost all credibility.
  - Newspapers were talking of a credibility gap between what he said and what he did.

- **Examples:**

- **Examples:**

  - inspire somebody to something His superb play inspired the team to a thrilling 5–0 win.
  - inspire somebody to do something By visiting schools, the actors hope to inspire children to put on their own productions.

### 3620. [mandatory](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/mandatory) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/mænˈdeɪtəri/` · **US:** `/ˈmændətɔːri/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/man/manda/mandatory__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/man/manda/mandatory__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अनिवार्य

### 3702. [notify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/notify) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnəʊtɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈnəʊtɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/notif/notify__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/notif/notify__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सूचित करना, सूचना देना
- **Examples:**
  - notify somebody Competition winners will be notified by post.
  - You must notify us in writing if you wish to cancel your subscription.
  - notify somebody of something The police must be notified of the date of the demonstration.
  - notify something to somebody The date of the demonstration must be notified to the police.

- **Meaning:** कुख्यात, बदनाम

### 4000. [recruit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/recruit_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈkruːt/` · **US:** `/rɪˈkruːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rec/recru/recruit__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rec/recru/recruit__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** नया  सदस्य, रंगरूट, नया सदस्य
- **Examples:**
  - the training of new recruits
  - He spoke of us scornfully as raw recruits (= people without training or experience).
